Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The np variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 415 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 827 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the np space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 415 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 827. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the np space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 665 and the convergence rate is O(702/n^5).

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 702. QED.
